| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001` | **ansible-tower competency rollup.** Subsystem: competency, entry served `Method::Uql`. The ontology answers one single-source and one cross-source question over the `ansible-tower` connector's access contract. The contract is fetched at query time, with no copy of the source. Children `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.1` and `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.2` hold the questions as entries of the table in MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9. Acceptance is the one table-driven served test; there is no per-connector acceptance row. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case` (L3) passes for entries `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.1` and `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.2`. |
| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.1` | **ansible-tower single-source question.** Subsystem: competency, query path. Caller chain: served `Method::Uql` (`crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_05.rs:119`) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/dispatch.rs::dispatch_sql_query` (:127) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/uql_statement.rs::handle_uql` (:17) → `Op::ForeignScan` in `crates/medulla-exec/src/exec/dispatch.rs` (:364) → `FederatedExec::foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/federated.rs:51`) → `foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/run.rs:26`) → `foreign_source_rows` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/mod.rs:96`) → replay of the recorded ansible-tower reply. Shape: case `ansible-tower.q1` in `tests/connector_competency/cases/ansible-tower.json`, read by `tests/connector_competency/cases.py::load_cases` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.7); recording `tests/connector_competency/recordings/ansible-tower/single.json` served by `tests/connector_competency/fixture_source.py::FixtureSource` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8); the engine side reuses `ForeignSourceRegistry` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation.rs:1531`) and adds no engine type; the abstention is `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` (`crates/medulla-identity-types/src/authority_audit.rs:31`). Question: `Which job templates failed in their last run, and against which inventory hosts?` Sources: `ansible-tower-mcp`; access contracts from CONN-ANSIBLE-TOWER-MCP-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R002. Rule: For each template take the run with the latest `finishedAt`. Keep it when `jobStatus` is `failed`. Answer columns: templateName, hostId, finishedAt. Vocabulary: `infrastructure-v1.ttl`; producer terms: `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R068.2` (AutomationJob), `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R068.3`, `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R068.4` (jobStatus), `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R068.5` (ranAgainstHost). Every answer row carries source, record id and as-of. Abstention: an unconnected source yields `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` naming it. Writes: none. Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5, CONN-ANSIBLE-TOWER-MCP-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R002. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[ansible-tower.q1]` (L3). It asserts the non-empty provenance-carrying answer on the recorded data and the typed abstention when one source is unregistered. |
Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5, connector repository: CONN-<connector>-CONTENT row for this competency case

| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R003.2` | **archivebox cross-source question.** Subsystem: competency, federated query. Caller chain: first served `Method::SourceIngest` (`crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_10.rs:112`) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline/native_routes.rs` (:399) writes the approved `owl:sameAs` links; then served `Method::Uql` (`crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_05.rs:119`) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/dispatch.rs::dispatch_sql_query` (:127) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/uql_statement.rs::handle_uql` (:17) → `Op::ForeignScan` in `crates/medulla-exec/src/exec/dispatch.rs` (:364) → `FederatedExec::foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/federated.rs:51`) → `foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/run.rs:26`) → the join across an approved link planned in `crates/medulla-federation/src/federation.rs` (MEDULLA-CQ-XSRC-R004.5) → replay of the recorded archivebox replies. Shape: case `archivebox.q2` in `tests/connector_competency/cases/archivebox.json`, read by `tests/connector_competency/cases.py::load_cases` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.7); recordings `tests/connector_competency/recordings/archivebox/cross_<source>.json` served by `tests/connector_competency/fixture_source.py::FixtureSource` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8); the link facts go in as one `SourceIngestionRequest` (`crates/medulla-source-types/src/source_ingestion.rs:268`); the engine side reuses `ForeignSourceRegistry` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation.rs:1531`) and adds no engine type; the abstention is `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` (`crates/medulla-identity-types/src/authority_audit.rs:31`). Question: `Which archived pages were first seen as FreshRSS articles but never captured by ArchiveBox?` The join concept is URL / web resource, and the other sources are `freshrss`, `searxng`. The harness first writes approved `owl:sameAs` links through served `Method::SourceIngest`. A join crosses only an approved link (MEDULLA-CQ-XSRC-R004.5). Each source has a recording `tests/connector_competency/recordings/archivebox/cross_<source>.json`. Each answer row carries provenance from every source it used. Rule: Anti-join `:Article` `:canonicalUrl` against `:WebSnapshot` `:resourceUrl` on the case-folded URL without fragment. Keep the articles with no snapshot. Search results of searxng join the same way. Answer columns: canonicalUrl, firstSeenAt. Vocabulary: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/content-v1.ttl`, terms `:Article`, `:canonicalUrl`; `crates/medulla-core/ontology/documentation-v1.ttl`, terms `:WebSnapshot`; `crates/medulla-core/ontology/retrieval-v1.ttl`, terms `:resourceUrl`, `:firstSeenAt`. Terms are already declared in the module. Case file: `tests/connector_competency/cases/archivebox.json`. Refusals: with one other source unconnected, the answer abstains. It names that source with reason `Unconnected` from `Method::SourceCoverage` (planned under MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1); it never returns an empty success. Writes: none at query time. Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R003.1, MEDULLA-CQ-XSRC-R004.5, MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[archivebox.q2]` (L3). It asserts joined rows with provenance. A second case drops one other source and asserts the named abstention. |

Shape: case `audio-transcriber.q1` in `tests/connector_competency/cases/audio-transcriber.json`, read by `tests/connector_competency/cases.py::load_cases` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.7); recording `tests/connector_competency/recordings/audio-transcriber/single.json` served by `tests/connector_competency/fixture_source.py::FixtureSource` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8); the engine side reuses `ForeignSourceRegistry` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation.rs:1531`) and adds no engine type; the abstention is `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` (`crates/medulla-identity-types/src/authority_audit.rs:31`). Question: `Which recordings were transcribed with a low-confidence segment, and what text was affected?` The case file holds one UQL statement over ontology classes only. It names no connector. The recording is `tests/connector_competency/recordings/audio-transcriber/single.json`. Rule: Keep `:TranscriptSegment` rows with `:segmentConfidence` below `confidence_floor` (table parameter 0.6). Follow `:segmentOf` to the `:Transcript` and `:transcribedFrom` to the recording. The affected text is read from the source record named by the row provenance. Answer columns: recordingId, segmentId, segmentConfidence. Each answer row carries `source`, `record_id` and `as_of`. The answer comes from the foreign read, never from a stored copy. Vocabulary: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/personal-v1.ttl`, terms `:Transcript`, `:TranscriptSegment`, `:segmentOf`, `:segmentConfidence`, `:transcribedFrom`. Terms are already declared in the module. Case file: `tests/connector_competency/cases/audio-transcriber.json`. Refusals: A missing pack import makes the engine refuse with `SOURCE_REGISTRATION_NOT_FOUND` (`crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/result.rs:299`). A held source refuses with `SOURCE_APPROVAL_NOT_ADMITTED`. `Method::SourceCoverage` (planned under MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1) then names the source with reason `Unconnected`. Writes: none. Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R073.1. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[audio-transcriber.q1]` (L3). It asserts a non-empty answer with provenance fields. A second case removes the pack and asserts the refusal code and the `Unconnected` audit. |

Shape: case `jellyfin.q1` in `tests/connector_competency/cases/jellyfin.json`, read by `tests/connector_competency/cases.py::load_cases` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.7); recording `tests/connector_competency/recordings/jellyfin/single.json` served by `tests/connector_competency/fixture_source.py::FixtureSource` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8); the engine side reuses `ForeignSourceRegistry` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation.rs:1531`) and adds no engine type; the abstention is `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` (`crates/medulla-identity-types/src/authority_audit.rs:31`). Question: `Which movies in a library have never been played and were added more than a year ago?` The case file holds one UQL statement over ontology classes only. It names no connector. The recording is `tests/connector_competency/recordings/jellyfin/single.json`. Rule: each answer row carries `source`, `record_id` and `as_of`. The answer comes from the foreign read, never from a stored copy. Vocabulary: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/media-v1.ttl`, terms `mv:Film`, `mv:PlaybackSession`, `mv:playCount`, `mv:addedAt` (MEDULLA-MEDIA-VOCAB-R001.1, R001.3, R001.4). Mapping owner: MEDULLA-MEDIA-VOCAB-R006.1. Case file: `tests/connector_competency/cases/jellyfin.json`. Refusals: A missing pack import makes the engine refuse with `SOURCE_REGISTRATION_NOT_FOUND`. A held source refuses with `SOURCE_APPROVAL_NOT_ADMITTED`. `Method::SourceCoverage` (planned under MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1) then names the source with reason `Unconnected`. Writes: none. Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R073.1. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[jellyfin.q1]` (L3). Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5

Caller chain: first served `Method::SourceIngest` (`crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_10.rs:112`) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline/native_routes.rs` (:399) writes the approved `owl:sameAs` links; then served `Method::Uql` (`crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_05.rs:119`) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/dispatch.rs::dispatch_sql_query` (:127) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/uql_statement.rs::handle_uql` (:17) → `Op::ForeignScan` in `crates/medulla-exec/src/exec/dispatch.rs` (:364) → `FederatedExec::foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/federated.rs:51`) → `foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/run.rs:26`) → the join across an approved link planned in `crates/medulla-federation/src/federation.rs` (MEDULLA-CQ-XSRC-R004.5) → replay of the recorded jellyfin replies. Shape: case `jellyfin.q2` in `tests/connector_competency/cases/jellyfin.json`, read by `tests/connector_competency/cases.py::load_cases` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.7); recordings `tests/connector_competency/recordings/jellyfin/cross_<source>.json` served by `tests/connector_competency/fixture_source.py::FixtureSource` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8); the link facts go in as one `SourceIngestionRequest` (`crates/medulla-source-types/src/source_ingestion.rs:268`); the engine side reuses `ForeignSourceRegistry` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation.rs:1531`) and adds no engine type; the abstention is `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` (`crates/medulla-identity-types/src/authority_audit.rs:31`). Each source has a recording `tests/connector_competency/recordings/jellyfin/cross_<source>.json`. Each answer row carries provenance from every source it used. Rule: Anti-join the work of each `:LibraryItem` (`:itemOf`) against the arr `:Film` and `:TelevisionSeries` rows on `:tmdbId` or `:tvdbId`. Keep the items with no match. Terms are already declared in the module. Case file: `tests/connector_competency/cases/jellyfin.json`. Refusals: with one other source unconnected, the answer abstains. It names that source with reason `Unconnected` from `Method::SourceCoverage` (planned under MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1); it never returns an empty success. Writes: none at query time. Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R027.1, MEDULLA-CQ-XSRC-R004.5, MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[jellyfin.q2]` (L3). Rule: For the table parameter `user`, follow `:assignedApp` from the user, from each `:hasRole` role and from each `:memberOfGroup` group. Return the client and the role or group it came through. Answer columns: userId, clientId, via. Each answer row carries `source`, `record_id` and `as_of`. The answer comes from the foreign read, never from a stored copy. Vocabulary: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/identity-v1.ttl`, terms `:IdentityUser`, `:hasRole`, `:IdentityRole`, `:memberOfGroup`, `:IdentityGroup`, `:assignedApp`. Terms are already declared in the module. Case file: `tests/connector_competency/cases/keycloak.json`. Refusals: A missing pack import makes the engine refuse with `SOURCE_REGISTRATION_NOT_FOUND` (`crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/result.rs:299`). A held source refuses with `SOURCE_APPROVAL_NOT_ADMITTED`. `Method::SourceCoverage` (planned under MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1) then names the source with reason `Unconnected`. Writes: none. Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R073.1. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[keycloak.q1]` (L3). It asserts a non-empty answer with provenance fields. A second case removes the pack and asserts the refusal code and the `Unconnected` audit. |

Question: `Which people have access to a service that has an open security incident?` The join concept is person / service, and the other sources are `okta`, `servicenow`. The harness first writes approved `owl:sameAs` links through served `Method::SourceIngest`. A join crosses only an approved link (MEDULLA-CQ-XSRC-R004.5). Each source has a recording `tests/connector_competency/recordings/keycloak/cross_<source>.json`. Each answer row carries provenance from every source it used. It names that source with reason `Unconnected` from `Method::SourceCoverage` (planned under MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1); it never returns an empty success. Writes: none at query time. Case file: `tests/connector_competency/cases/mattermost.json`. Refusals: with one other source unconnected, the answer abstains. It names that source with reason `Unconnected` from `Method::SourceCoverage` (planned under MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1); it never returns an empty success. Writes: none at query time. Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R036.1, MEDULLA-CQ-XSRC-R004.5, MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R004.6.1. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[mattermost.q2]` (L3). | `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R041.1` | **objectstore single-source question.** Question: `Which buckets hold the most data and which objects have not been read in a year?` Stage: competency over the `objectstore` connector, entry served `Uql`. Caller chain: served `Method::Uql` (`crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_05.rs:119`) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/dispatch.rs::dispatch_sql_query` (:127) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/uql_statement.rs::handle_uql` (:17) → `Op::ForeignScan` in `crates/medulla-exec/src/exec/dispatch.rs` (:363) → `FederatedExec::foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/federated.rs:51`) → `foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/run.rs:26`) → `foreign_source_rows` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/mod.rs:96`) → replay of the recorded objectstore reply. Shape: case `objectstore.q1` in `tests/connector_competency/cases/objectstore.json`, read by `tests/connector_competency/cases.py::load_cases` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.7); recording `tests/connector_competency/recordings/objectstore/single.json` served by `tests/connector_competency/fixture_source.py::FixtureSource` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8); the engine side reuses `ForeignSourceRegistry` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation.rs:1531`) and leaves the engine types unchanged; the abstention is `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` (`crates/medulla-identity-types/src/authority_audit.rs:31`). Vocabulary: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/data_platform-v1.ttl`, terms `dp:Bucket`, `dp:StoredObject`, `dp:inStore`, `dp:byteSize`, `dp:lastModified` (MEDULLA-DATAPLAT-VOCAB-R003.1); the object store exposes the modification time, so "not read in a year" is a `dp:lastModified` older than 365 days before the fetch time. This is an approximation: the object store does not expose the read time. Graph pattern: `?o a dp:StoredObject ; dp:inStore ?b ; dp:byteSize ?n ; dp:lastModified ?m`; `?b a dp:Bucket`; the buckets are ordered by the sum of `?n`; the stale objects satisfy `?m < ?asOf - "P365D"^^xsd:duration`. The case lists the single-source set: objectstore. Rule: Return buckets by descending total bytes and, for each, its stale objects. The source is read at query time and no source row is stored. Each answer row carries connector, contract IRI, mapping IRI and fetch time (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5). The answer columns are bucket, totalBytes, staleObjectKey. A wrong answer is a failure, not an abstention. Refusals: `Unconnected` names the missing source (MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R006.2). `SOURCE_HELD` names a held mapping. `SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE` flags a down source (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.4). Evidence: the `AuthorityAudit` of MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R007.1 lists each source used and its reason. Writes: none; `Uql` is read-only. Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R007.1, MEDULLA-DATAPLAT-VOCAB-R003.1, MEDULLA-DATAPLAT-VOCAB-R006.3. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[objectstore.q1]` (L3) asserts the columns, a non-empty answer, the provenance fields and zero stored source rows. `test_case_abstains[objectstore.q1]` (L3) asserts `Unconnected` naming each source when its mapping is absent. |

| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R045.1` | **owncast single-source question.** Question: `Which streams had the highest peak viewer count and how long did they run?` Stage: competency over the `owncast` connector, entry served `Uql`. Caller chain: served `Method::Uql` (`crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_05.rs:119`) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/dispatch.rs::dispatch_sql_query` (:127) → `crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/uql_statement.rs::handle_uql` (:17) → `Op::ForeignScan` in `crates/medulla-exec/src/exec/dispatch.rs` (:363) → `FederatedExec::foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/federated.rs:51`) → `foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/run.rs:26`) → `foreign_source_rows` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation_opt/mod.rs:96`) → replay of the recorded owncast reply. Shape: case `owncast.q1` in `tests/connector_competency/cases/owncast.json`, read by `tests/connector_competency/cases.py::load_cases` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.7); recording `tests/connector_competency/recordings/owncast/single.json` served by `tests/connector_competency/fixture_source.py::FixtureSource` (MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8); the engine side reuses `ForeignSourceRegistry` (`crates/medulla-federation/src/federation.rs:1531`) and leaves the engine types unchanged; the abstention is `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` (`crates/medulla-identity-types/src/authority_audit.rs:31`). Vocabulary: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/media-v1.ttl`, terms `:BroadcastStream`, `:viewerCount`, `:ViewerSample`, `:sampleOf`, `:sampledAt` (MEDULLA-MEDIA-VOCAB-R651); the case does not use `:peakViewerCount`, whose domain is `:LiveStream`. Graph pattern: `?s a :BroadcastStream ; :viewerCount ?peak`; `?v a :ViewerSample ; :sampleOf ?s ; :sampledAt ?t`. The case lists the single-source set: owncast. Rule: Order streams by `?peak` descending. The run time is the latest minus the earliest `:sampledAt` of the stream samples. The source is read at query time and no source row is stored. Each answer row carries connector, contract IRI, mapping IRI and fetch time (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5). The answer columns are stream, peakViewers, runSeconds. A wrong answer is a failure, not an abstention. Refusals: `Unconnected` names the missing source (MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R006.2). `SOURCE_HELD` names a held mapping. `SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE` flags a down source (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.4). Evidence: the `AuthorityAudit` of MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R007.1 lists each source used and its reason. Writes: none; `Uql` is read-only. Depends: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R007.1, MEDULLA-MEDIA-VOCAB-R651. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[owncast.q1]` (L3) asserts the columns, a non-empty answer, the provenance fields and zero stored source rows. `test_case_abstains[owncast.q1]` (L3) asserts `Unconnected` naming each source when its mapping is absent. |

The case lists the cross-source set: owncast and postiz. Mattermost is dropped from the source list: no mattermost term links a message to a stream. Rule: the source is read at query time and no source row is stored. Each answer row carries connector, contract IRI, mapping IRI and fetch time (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5). Shape: two fixture mappings in the test, one without and one with `vg:materialisation accelerated`, `vg:materialisationReason`, `vg:freshnessMaxAgeSeconds`; the shape check is the one of MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R002.5; no engine type is added. Rule: a source without `vg:materialisation` is `virtual` and stores no row. A source with `vg:materialisation accelerated`, a `vg:materialisationReason`, a `vg:freshnessMaxAgeSeconds` and the connection capability `copy` may keep a watermark-bound cache. The cache is never the source of record. Refusals: the shape of MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R002.5 rejects an accelerated row without its reason, freshness bound or `copy` capability. Writes: only the declared cache. Depends: MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R001.5, MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R002.5, MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_contract_kinds_served.py::test_6_materialisation` (L3) asserts no stored row without the statement and a watermark-bound cache with it. |
| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.7` | **Case table schema and loader.** Stage: test support for competency. Code: `tests/connector_competency/cases.py::load_cases` reads `tests/connector_competency/cases/<connector>.json`. Each file holds `connector`, `rollup` and a `questions` list. Each file also holds `vocabulary_module` and `terms`, the term IRIs its questions use. Each question holds `id`, `kind` (`single` or `cross`), `uql`, `sources`, `optional_sources`, `columns`, `min_rows` and `recordings`. Shape: `cases.py` holds a `Case` dataclass with the keys listed above and raises `CaseInvalid` carrying the code `CASE_INVALID`; `test_cases_schema.py` and `test_competency_served.py` import it; no engine code is touched. Writes: none. Depends: none. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_cases_schema.py::test_every_rollup_has_a_valid_case_file` (L1) loads every file and asserts each `CASE_INVALID` variant. |

| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R089` | **Served vocabulary coverage test for the modules of R071 to R081.** Stage: ontology and packs, then query. Entry: served `ConnectorPack.Import`. Caller chain: served `ConnectorPackOp::Import` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:53`) → `serve_import` (:146) → `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/connector_pack_projection.rs::project` (:286) → served `Method::SourceIngest` (`crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_10.rs:112`) → `crates/medulla-h-ingest/src/source_ingestion.rs::prepare` (:156) → served `Method::Sparql` → `crates/medulla-h-sparql/src/rdf/sparql.rs::handle_sparql` (:32). Shape: one new table-driven test file `tests/typed_packs/test_connector_vocabulary_served.py`; no new Rust type. One table-driven test with one case per term of R071 to R081, keyed by the term name. Each case imports a fixture pack whose ontology class extends the term's class. It ingests one recorded record of that class through served `SourceIngest`. It reads the record back with served `Uql` on its type and the node properties, because a source record is a property-graph node (`source:<digest>` id), not an RDF resource, and so is not in the SPARQL projection; the term's declaration is proved by the import itself (a pack class extending a term the catalog lacks is refused `ONTOLOGY_ORPHAN`). It checks source, record id and as-of in the row, and for a property term the value under the term IRI. Shape cases also send a violating record through served `ShaclValidate` (`crates/medulla-h-sparql/src/rdf/validation.rs::handle_shacl_validate`) and expect the shape message. Commercial cases resolve the `MEDULLA-SUPPLY-VOCAB-R003.7` to `R003.12` IRIs for erpnext, salesforce and twenty through registered modules. Refusal: a pack class without the term is flagged `ONTOLOGY_ORPHAN` (`MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R105.1`). A record that lacks a property term stores no value under the term (`test_record_lacking_the_property_stores_no_value`), and `test_pack_class_without_the_term_is_an_orphan` proves the `ONTOLOGY_ORPHAN` refusal. Records written: the imported pack and the ingested records only. Depends: `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R105.1`, `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R071` to `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R081`. | L4: `tests/typed_packs/test_connector_vocabulary_served.py::test_pack_record_of_each_new_term_round_trips[<term>]` passes for every term case against a served engine. |

Shape: new test `tests/federated/test_virtual_graph_federation_served.py` reusing the registry above; no new Rust type. A source that the caller cannot read adds no rows and appears in the coverage block (`MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R006.1`). Refusal: a query with a write stage must refuse before any source is called. Records written: none. Companion scenario: `ARBORA-ACCEPTANCE-R006.7`.
